| wp-explicit-clone-en | en | pass | judge: One English ack names the action (cloning and fixing on hel), says it will report when clear, and gives no duration or question. A single task is opened on hel for nowledge-co/oomol-cli, not the Mac, and nothing claims the typo is already fixed. The extra cloning line is Ducter's own by design, so it doesn't count against the partner. |
| wp-retry-old-missing-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner acks the retry in one short Chinese line that names the action, with no question and no duration. The clone line is Ducter's own by design. The task runs on hel against nowledge-co/mem, clones first, and never touches the Mac. No path or error is shown to the user. |
| wp-agy-tools-fit-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent one short English ack that names the action (fixing the typo with Antigravity on the Mac) and says it will report back, with no question and no invented duration. It then opened one task on the local Mac in repo wey-gu/foo, assigned to Antigravity, and claimed no result. |
| wp-phone-update-fix-zh | zh | **fail** | says /\u53EB\u5230\u4E86\|\u5DF2\u7ECF\u53EB\|booked/; judge: The partner sent one short calm Chinese line that names the action (update Didi, then retry) without asking the user anything or claiming a booking. It told the existing taxi task via task_send to update from the Play Store and retry, and did not paste the English instruction to the user or open a new task. |
| wp-own-agent-quota-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent one truthful Bridge reply saying Claude Code is out of its usage limit until 23:00 today, opened no task, and asked the user nothing. The user-facing note is a brief status update, not a question or a new task, and it stays in English. |
| wp-mem-contradiction-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner flags the conflict in Chinese with 你, names the remembered Thursday 3pm change, asks which day is correct, and sets no reminder. The reply runs two sentences rather than the single short line the rubric example suggests, and it says 上周 where the memory was two days old, but neither is a rule violation. |
| wp-bound-computer-away-zh | zh | pass | judge: The only step is a background 'bench' task failing to start on hel, and no partner message is visible, so silence is valid. Nothing is shown opening on the Mac, the user isn't asked anything, and no false claim about hel or the benchmark appears. |
| wp-no-copy-no-computer-en | en | pass | judge: The partner gave one short English ack naming the action, with no duration and no question. The 'cloning it on hel first' line matches the rubric's allowed Ducter-own exception. It opened one task on hel with the repo cloned there, and it did not claim the typo is fixed. |
| wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh | zh | pass | judge: The retry runs on hel, the partner's home computer, via the 'local' place, so nothing runs on the Mac. Codex takes over without asking the user, and the user hears one short Chinese ack that names the action (换个代理接着修) and says when it will report. The agent's quota error is not pasted and the 4-hour wait is not mentioned. |
| wp-other-task-names-owned-pr-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner stayed silent after the review task reported the #10932 playwright failure. The rubric allows silence, and the owning copy task already receives that report, so no new task, question, or false claim appears. |
| wp-task-wait-ended-ask-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is two short Chinese sentences. It says the copy task is still working with no result yet, and reports the last readable CI look (2 of 3 done, one in progress), which matches the rubric. It opens no task, asks nothing, promises no outcome, and does not state CI as passed or failed. |
| wp-long-round-ci-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The message is two sentences, not one short line, and it misstates the steps: they show two of three checks green with e2e still running, but it says three other checks passed. It asks nothing, doesn't claim CI passed, and doesn't imply it is stuck, so the count error and extra length are what fail it. |
| wp-long-round-stuck-en | en | pass | judge: The partner told the task to stop retrying and report the GitHub sign-in on hel as expired and needing renewal, and told the user in one short line what it saw (access expired, CI stuck since 5:25 pm) and that renewing it is theirs. It claims nothing beyond the repeated 401s and sends no code changes or pushes. |
| wp-changes-requested-before-wait-zh | zh | **fail** | judge: The reply runs to four sentences, over the three-sentence limit, and it repeats the stale 'only approval missing / CI all green' claim before correcting it. It also never tells the user the task has the requested change or nudges the task with task_send; it only says it will keep watching. |
| wp-partner-meant-for-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner forwarded the message to 小家 via the partner tool, set no task or reminder itself, asked nothing, and replied with one short Chinese line using 你 saying it went to 小家. It did not claim 小家 had already done anything. |
| wp-partner-other-owns-task-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is one short Chinese line that uses 你-free phrasing, says plainly it has no dentist booking record here, points the question to 小家, and offers to pass it on. It does not state or guess whether the appointment is booked and opens no task. |
| wp-partner-make-zh | zh | pass | judge: 小家was created immediately with no questions, opening a task, or settings explanation. The reply is short, in 简体中文 with 你, and states its scope in the user's terms (管家里的事) and how to talk to it (点它 in the partner list). It omits the left/‹ location detail, which is a minor gap, so it passes. |
| wp-partner-brain-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner called brain_switch to move 小玛 to Opus, not its own model, and replied with one short Chinese line saying 小玛 uses Opus from the next message. It did not switch itself, forward the request as a message, open a task, or ask first. The extra clause about memory and conversations being unchanged is a minor, plausible claim that doesn't break a rule. |
| wp-partner-brain-self-en | en | pass | judge: The partner used brain_switch and sent one short English line saying it will think with Opus from the next message. It did not claim to change Ducter's setting, open a task, or ask a question, so it meets the rubric. |
| wp-partner-pass-back-en | en | pass | judge: The user asked Home to pass a message to Ducter; Home sent it (the partner tool call is the forward) without opening any task or acting on the release, and replied in English with one short line saying it went to Ducter and Ducter will answer in its own conversation. It does not claim Ducter's answer or ask a question, so it meets the rubric. |
| wp-mem-no-cant-lesson-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner treated the timeout as a one-off ('这次请重试') and retried at once with one short Chinese ack, with no memory save and no claim that hel or Codex can't browse. The brief frames the failure as this attempt only, so it passes. |
| wp-mem-question-not-fact-en | en | pass | judge: The partner answered the general question in one English sentence, with no task opened and no memory saved. The sentence is somewhat long for "short", but it stays within the one-or-two-sentence limit. |
| wp-thread-unnamed-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner answers directly in Chinese with the race cause (test queried before the index was built), the fix (await indexReady() before the first query), and the Nov 12 Codex origin, in two short sentences. It doesn't mention searching or paste the conversation, so it matches the rubric. |
| wp-promise-kept-zh | zh | pass | judge: Step 1 gets a single Chinese ack naming the search action with no invented duration, then a task is opened. Step 2 gives both options with their yen prices in brief Chinese, and it honestly flags that the claims are unverified. The message is one run-on paragraph rather than a few short lines, and it doesn't note that the task fell short of three candidates, but the rubric's pass condition is met. |
| wp-loop-posts-reviews-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner recorded the standing go with the without_asking tool and replied with one short Chinese line using 你: reviews post directly from now on, and saying so takes one sentence to revoke. It asked nothing, made no card, opened no task, and did not describe confirmation or call anything a rule. |
| wp-babysitter-test-after-dont-ask-zh | zh | pass | judge: At the second flaky-check ask, the partner answered Go on the user's earlier permission without asking again and showed no user-facing question, so silence is allowed. Its earlier line promised only in-task reruns and PR comments, and said merge still needs the user's nod, which is accurate. |
| wp-babysitter-merge-still-asks-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner does not merge on the user's behalf. It says in two short Chinese sentences that checks passed and ThaddeusJiang approved, then asks whether to merge to main, which keeps the irreversible merge with the user. Restating the question in prose slightly duplicates the buttons, but the rubric asks for exactly this ask. |
| wp-pr-babysitter-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner made the standing agent at once with keep_make, sent no task, loop, timer or question, and replied in one short Chinese line that it is looking after Nowledge Mem PR babysitter and that the user's agents hand PRs to it by that name. It names no Settings pane and makes no false claim about Bridge waking. The address and brief can't be seen in the visible tool output, so they are not verified. |
| wp-keep-fare-watch-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner replied with one short Chinese line (好，我来照看：…) covering when it looks (每天早上 9 点), when it speaks (under 2000), and when it stops (订了就停), and used keep_make. The task it opened carries the watch's own brief, which the rubric allows, and it asks no question, shows no card, and claims no current fare. |
| wp-keep-fare-change-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner used keep_change on the existing watch instead of creating a second one, and its one short Chinese line uses 你 and states the new 1800 threshold. It opened no task and asked no question, and the 2000 rule is no longer standing. The remark that the current round is still running is a minor status claim but doesn't invent results. |
| wp-keep-fare-stop-zh | zh | pass | judge: At the last message the partner calls keep_change and replies with one short Chinese line confirming the ticket is booked and it has stopped watching. The tool arguments aren't shown, so I'm assuming the call was stop rather than pause. |
| wp-keep-weekly-en | en | pass | judge: The partner made the schedule at once with keep_make, opened no task, asked no question, and showed no card. Its reply is one short English line starting with the expected 'Done, I'll look after it:' and describes the weekly draft without explaining how scheduling works. |
| wp-at-task-followup-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent the Safari verification and test request to the finished login-redirect task, not the running bench task or a new one. Its one Chinese ack line says it is on it, uses 你, names the action without claiming Safari already passes, and gives no invented duration. |
| wp-slash-skill-en | en | pass | judge: One task was opened in the wey-gu/foo repo on the user's Mac, and its brief directs the worker to use the /kill-ai-slop skill on the landing page. The user-facing ack is one short English sentence that names the action, with no question and no explanation of the skill. The skill description appears only inside the internal brief, not in the user's view. |

## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。

比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你订酒店、查机票、看邮件和日程。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a failing test in one of your repos fixed and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 맡아서 진행하고 결과를 알려드려요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 그때 이어서 얘기하면 됩니다.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは信頼性より速さを優先する通信です。TCPは届いたか・順番どおりかを確認して再送しますが、UDPは確認も再送もせず、データをそのまま送ります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama privada al día con la principal y limpiar tus commits antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir su historial rompe el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据有序、不丢不重地到达；UDP 不做这些保证，只管尽快发出去，速度快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase to update your own unpublished branch with the latest base branch, so its history reads as one clean line. Merge when the branch is shared with others or you want the history to show exactly when the two lines met.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符串的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "某个用例的名字"
```

决定：L0 文件数触发阈值做成可配置项，默认值保持 8，行为与现在一致。改完先给用户看，不推送、不合并、不开 PR（用户昨天说过「改好先给我看，别推」）。

完成标准：
- 阈值可通过配置（选一个仓库里已有的配置方式，比如配置结构体字段、环境变量或配置文件，沿用项目现有风格）设置，未设置时默认 8。
- 增加或更新测试：默认值为 8；设置为其他值（比如 4、16）后触发时机随之变化；非法值（比如 0 或负数）有明确处理。
- 全部测试通过，报告里给出测试结果。
- 改动提交在当前分支 ducter/compaction-l0 上，报告里说明改了哪些文件和新配置项的名字与用法。

范围外：不改 compaction 的其他逻辑，不推送，不合并到主分支。

地点：~/de
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it intends to make, before touching the actual data files. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to finish or undo the interrupted changes, so committed data survives and nothing is left half-written.
```

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1923 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Waiting for the first run</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">Writes/s and p99 latency show here after each run</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">No runs yet. Each night's result lands here.</div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  function render(runs) {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!runs || !runs.length) { empty.style.display = ''; return; }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    runs.forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + ' writes/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('s').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  }
  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (v) {
      render(v && v.runs ? v.runs : (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []));
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>
</main>
<style>
.k-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 10px; }
.k-row { padding: 10px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--d-line); }
.k-row-main { font-weight: 600; color: var(--d-ink); }
.k-row-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--d-muted); }
.k-row-value { font-size: 15px; color: var(--d-accent); }
.k-none { color: var(--d-muted); }

```

  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 4493 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 合计</div>
    <div id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>

  <div class="k-h">按天</div>
  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>第几天</label>
      <div class="k-chips" id="dayChips"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>花在哪</label>
      <input class="k-input" id="item" placeholder="比如 午饭、打车">
    </div>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label>多少钱</label>
      <input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0.00">
    </div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var DAYS = 7;
  var items = [];
  var day = 1;
  var openIdx = -1;

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function money(n) { return '¥' + Number(n).toFixed(2); }

  function total() {
    return items.reduce(function (s, it) { return s + it.amt; }, 0);
  }

  function save() {
    ducter.set('bj-expenses', it … document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      items.splice(Number(del.getAttribute('data-del')), 1);
      openIdx = -1;
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) {
      var i = Number(row.getAttribute('data-i'));
      openIdx = openIdx === i ? -1 : i;
      render();
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var name = document.getElementById('item').value.trim();
    var amtEl = document.getElementById('amt');
    var amt = parseFloat(amtEl.value);
    if (!name || isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) return;
    items.push({ day: day, item: name, amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100 });
    save();
    document.getElementById('item').value = '';
    amtEl.value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').removeAttribute('open');
    render();
  });

  function load(v) {
    items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('bj-expenses')).then(load);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('bj-expenses')).then(load);
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(sum);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum):"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.textContent="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(sum):"还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done. The page is on your shelf: four PRs, with a tick for each as it lands.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2408 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting on someone</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { align-items: center; }
  .k-row input[type=checkbox] { width: 20px; height: 20px; margin-right: 12px; accent-color: var(--d-accent); flex: none; }
  .k-row.is-landed .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-row-main { min-width: 0; }
  .k-row-value { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; white-space: nowrap; }
  .k-row-value.bad { color: var(--d-bad); }
</style>
<script>
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];

  function landed() { return ducter.get('landed') || []; }

  function render() {
    var done = landed();
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    PRS.forEach(function (pr) {
      var isDone = done.indexOf(pr.id) !== -1;
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'k-row' + (isDone ? ' is-landed' : '');

      var box = document.createElement('input');
      box.type = 'checkbox';
      box.checked = isDone;
      box.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Landed: ' + pr.repo);
      box.onchange = function () {
        var cur = landed().filter(function (x) { return x !== pr.id; });
        if (box.checked) cur.push(pr.id);
        ducter.set('landed', cur);
        render();
      };

      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.innerHTML = '<div>' + pr.title + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + pr.repo + '</div>';

      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value' + (pr.tone ? ' ' + pr.tone : '');
      val.textContent = isDone ? 'Landed' : pr.wait;

      li.appendChild(box);
      li.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });

    var openCount = PRS.filter(function (pr) { return done.indexOf(pr.id) === -1; }).length;
    document.getElementById('open').textContent = openCount;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = done.length + ' landed · tick each one off as it lands';
    ducter.summary(openCount + ' open, ' + done.length + ' landed');
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday","tone":""},{"id":"m10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","wait":"On CI, failing","tone":"bad"},{"id":"h1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","tone":""},{"id":"d418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","wait":"Draft, not sent for review","tone":""}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  [partner message] Usually yes: pnpm's shared content store and workspace-aware installs tend to make big monorepo installs faster and lighter on disk than npm, though the gap varies with cache state and project layout, so benchmark your own repo.
